AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='The fix is complete and verified to compile.\n\n## Root cause\n\nIn `ImageEditor.java`, the navigation guard was:\n\n```java\nif (mClicked == mClickedFilter) { /* navigate */ } else { toast("Save Current Image First"); }\n```\n\nThis compares two unrelated flags: `mClicked` ("Save current was pressed") and `mClickedFilter` ("a non-NONE filter was applied"). For the reported scenario:\n\n| Step | `mClicked` | `mClickedFilter` | Guard |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Brush → draw | false | false | equal → ok |\n| Save current | **true** | **false** | **unequal → toast** ❌ |\n\nBecause a doodle-only edit never sets `mClickedFilter`, saving made the two flags diverge, so the "Save current first" toast appeared *after* the image had already been saved. (Symmetrically, the old logic also let a user navigate away with an unsaved doodle before saving, since both flags were then `false`.)\n\n## The fix\n\nReplaced the flag-equality heuristic with an explicit unsaved-changes state, `mUnsavedChanges`:\n\n- **Set `true`** when a real edit happens: a non-NONE filter is applied, or the `OnPhotoEditorListener` reports an added/changed view (doodle stroke, text, sticker). A new `createPhotoEditor()` helper centralizes editor construction so the listener is attached at all three places the editor was rebuilt.\n- **Set `false`** in `saveAsFile`\'s `onSuccess` — the image is on disk, so navigation is free (this is the actual bug fix). It stays `true` on save failure, so the warning is still correct.\n- **Set `false`** in `resetCurrent()`, since reverting to the original leaves nothing to save.\n- **Cleared on navigation** via `resetEditingState()`, which also keeps `mDoodleSelected` in sync with whether brush mode is still active, so saving on the next image doesn\'t rebuild the editor and wipe fresh strokes.\n\nAlso initialized `mFilterName` to `PhotoFilter.NONE.name()` so a doodle-only save no longer produces a `...null.png` filename, and removed the now-unused `mClicked` field.\n\n## Verification\n\n`./gradlew clean assembleDebug` cannot run here: this sandbox has no network and Android Gradle Plugin 3.1.3 (required by `build.gradle`) is absent from the local Gradle cache, so the build fails while resolving the plugin itself — before any project code is touched, and identically with the pristine sources.\n\nTo verify compilation regardless, I type-checked `ImageEditor.java` and its transitive project dependencies with `javac` against the real `android-27` SDK jar and the project\'s cached AAR/JAR dependencies (using a generated stub `R` class and a stub for the one uncached third-party type, `com.itextpdf.text.Rectangle`).
